## Topic 4: Plane in Space

### Q1. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>, which is perpendicular to line <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> is passing through the point at which the above line meets the plane <math><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>21</mn></math>, then the distance of plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#160;</mo></math>from origin is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** 5
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>32</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>37</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>37</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Any general point on the line is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2,</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

This point most satisfy <math><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>21</mn></math>

Hence <math><mn>2</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>21</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>

So, the point of intersection of line and plane is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>)</mo></math>

So, equation of plane P = 0 is <math><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>10</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>(</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>(</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>37</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

Hence, distance of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> from origin is

<math><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>37</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>37</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q2. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mn>2</mn></math> planes are being contained by the vectors <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#945;</mi><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>.</mo></math> If the angle between these <math><mn>2</mn></math> planes is <math><mi>&#952;</mi></math>, then the value of <math><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>15</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>17</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>289</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>717</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>289</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2151</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>17</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2151</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>289</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2151</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

All given vectors are coplaner
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mi>&#945;</mi></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>5</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>10</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>5</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>12</mn><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>12</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>9</mn><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
A normal vector to the plane is <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>5</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>12</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>8</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>, then a normal vector to one of the plane is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><msub><mi>n</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math>, then a normal vector to the other plane is <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>11</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>13</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#952;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mover><msub><mi>n</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#183;</mo><mover><msub><mi>n</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mover><msub><mi>n</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mover><msub><mi>n</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>11</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>13</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><msqrt><mn>121</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>16</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>169</mn><mn>4</mn></mfrac></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>17</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><msqrt><mn>717</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>289</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2151</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math>

</details>

### Q3. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math> be the foot of the perpendicular from the origin to the plane <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and <math><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></math><math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> be a point on the plane. Then, the length of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><msqrt><mn>13</mn></msqrt></math> units
- **(B)** <math><msqrt><mn>15</mn></msqrt></math> units
- **(C)** <math><mn>4</mn></math> units
- **(D)** <math><msqrt><mn>17</mn></msqrt></math> units

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><msqrt><mn>13</mn></msqrt></math> units**

**Detailed Solution:**

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/7a32efef-ae41-4d2b-9d0a-51c06979edcc.png" style="width:2.23333in;height:1.92014in" />
From the diagram
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>16</mn></msqrt><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>17</mn></msqrt></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
<math><mo>∵</mo><mo>∆</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></math> is right angled triangle
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>=</mo><msqrt><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></msqrt><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>13</mn></msqrt></math> units

</details>

### Q4. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A plane <math><mi>P</mi></math> passes through the point <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1,1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and is parallel to the vectors <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math> and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>. The distance of the point <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>3</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> from the plane is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></math> units
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> units
- **(C)** <math><mn>3</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></math> units
- **(D)** <math><mn>3</mn></math> units

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> units**

**Detailed Solution:**

Equation of the plane is <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
The distance of point <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> from <math><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> is
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> units

</details>

### Q5. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The plane <math><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math> is rotated about its line of intersection with the <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>xy</mi><mo>-</mo></math>plane by an acute angle <math><mi mathvariant="normal">&#945;</mi></math> . If the new position of the plane contains the point <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3,1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>, then the value of <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mrow></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Let the equation of the plane be</p><p><math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8757;</mo></math> it contains the point <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3,1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtext>&#8658;6</mtext><mo>&#8211;</mo><mtext>2</mtext><mo>+</mo><mtext>1</mtext><mo>&#8211;</mo><mtext>3</mtext><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
Hence, the equation of the plane is
<math><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mn>2</mn></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></msqrt><msqrt><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>7</mn><mn>9</mn></mfrac></math></p>

</details>

### Q6. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The plane <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> intersect <math><mi>x</mi></math>-axis, <math><mi>y</mi></math>-axis and <math><mi>z</mi></math>-axis at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mi>B</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>C</mi></math> respectively. If the distance between the origin and the centroid of <math><mo>∆</mo><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><mi>C</mi></math> is <math><msub><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math> units and the volume of the tetrahedron <math><mi>O</mi><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><mi>C</mi></math> is <math><msub><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> cubic units, then the value of <math><mfrac><mrow><msubsup><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></math> is equal to (where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>O</mi></math> is the origin)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>21</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>14</mn><mn>9</mn></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>63</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>14</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>14</mn><mn>9</mn></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> cuts <math><mi>x</mi></math> -axis, <math><mi>y</mi></math> -axis, <math><mi>z</mi></math> -axis at <math><mi>A</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>B</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>C</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> respectively
Centroid of <math><mo>∆</mo><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><mi>C</mi></math> is <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
Distance between centroid and origin is <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></msqrt></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><msub><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><mn>14</mn></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> units
Volume of tetrahedron <math><mi>O</mi><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><mi>C</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>×</mo><mi>O</mi><mi>A</mi><mo>×</mo><mi>O</mi><mi>B</mi><mo>×</mo><mi>O</mi><mi>C</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>k</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>6</mn></mfrac><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> cubic unit
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><msubsup><mi>k</mi><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></msubsup><msub><mi>k</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mfrac><mn>14</mn><mn>9</mn></mfrac><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>14</mn><mn>9</mn></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q7. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

Two lines <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> and <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> intersect at a point <math><mi>P</mi><mo>.</mo></math> If the distance of <math><mi>P</mi></math> from the plane <math><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>7</mn></math> is <math><mi>λ</mi></math> units, then the value of <math><mn>49</mn><mi>λ</mi></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **84**

**Detailed Solution:**

Point <math><mi>P</mi></math> must lies on both the given lines
From <math><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow></msup></math> line coordinate of point <math><mi>P</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1,3</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2,4</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
This must satisfy <math><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi></mrow></msup></math> line
i.e. <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mn>5</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>15</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
So, coordinates of point <math><mi>P</mi></math> are <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
Hence, the distance of <math><mi>P</mi></math> from the plane is
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow><mn>7</mn></mfrac></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>12</mn><mn>7</mn></mfrac></math> units

</details>

### Q8. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The sum of the intercepts of the plane on the coordinate axes, passing through the intersection of the planes <math><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and <math><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and parallel to the line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>, is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>11</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>105</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>11</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>102</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>101</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>11</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>102</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Equation of the plane passing through the line of intersection of the <math><mn>2</mn></math> given planes is <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>λ</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi>x</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>λ</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>λ</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>λ</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>λ</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>∵</mo><mi></mi></math> This plane is parallel to <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>λ</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>λ</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>λ</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>λ</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>15</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>25</mn><mi>λ</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>21</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>21</mn><mi>λ</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mn>8</mn><mi>λ</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>32</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mi></mi><mo>⇒</mo><mi>λ</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>
So, equation of the plane is <math><mn>10</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>17</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>15</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
Sum of intercepts <math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>17</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>15</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>51</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>30</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>34</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>510</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>55</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>510</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>11</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>102</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q9. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> be three planes. The distance of the line of intersection of planes <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> from the plane <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>26</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math> units
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>26</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math> units
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>26</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math> units
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>26</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math> units

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>26</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math> units**

**Detailed Solution:**

A vector parallel to the line of intersection is
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mtable><mtr><mtd><mo>=</mo></mtd><mtd><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mfenced separators="|"><mn>3</mn></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mfenced separators="|"><mn>1</mn></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></mtd></mtr></mtable></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mo>=</mo></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd></mtr></mtable></math>
for a point on the line of intersection
Put <math><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="" close="}" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
So, equation line to the intersection is
<math><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>&#8757;</mo></math> line of intersection is parallel to the plane <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
So, distance <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow><msqrt><mn>26</mn></msqrt></mfrac></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>4</mn><msqrt><mn>26</mn></msqrt></mfrac></math>

</details>

---

### Q10. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If the lines <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>L</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>:</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>λ</mi></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math> and <math><msub><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>:</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> are coplanar, then the equation of the plane passing through the point of intersection of <math><msub><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math> and <math><msub><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> which is at a maximum distance from the origin is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>50</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>50</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mo>∵</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><msub><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math> and <math><msub><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> are coplanar, hence, 
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>λ</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>⇒</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>λ</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
Now point of intersection will be <math><mi>P</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>∴</mo></math> Equation of required plane is <math><mn>4</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>50</mn></math>

</details>

### Q11. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Consider the  planes <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>:</mo><mo> </mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>:</mo><mo> </mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math> having normals <math><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>N</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math> and <math><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>N</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> respectively. The distance of the origin from the plane passing through the point <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1,1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and whose normal is perpendicular to <math><msub><mrow><mi>N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math> and <math><msub><mrow><mi>N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>5</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math> units
- **(B)** <math><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></math> units
- **(C)** <math><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></math> units
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>14</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>35</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math> units

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></math> units**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>N</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math> (given)
<math><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>N</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math> (given)
<math><mo>∵</mo></math> normal of required plane is <math><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>N</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>×</mo><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>N</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
So, equation of the required plane is
<math><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
So, distance from <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0,0</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> is <math><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>35</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></math>

</details>

### Q12. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The plane <math><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>81</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> is rotated through a right angle about its line of intersection with the plane <math><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>10</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>25</mn><mo>.</mo></math> If the equation of the plane in its new position is <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mo>,</mo></math> then the value of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>106</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>89</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>73</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>37</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mn>106</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

The equation of the plane through the line of intersection of the planes is
<math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>81</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>10</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>25</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>7</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>10</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>81</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>25</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
Which is parallel to <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>10</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi></mrow><mn>6</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>81</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>25</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
So, <math><mo>-</mo><mn>16</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>20</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>7</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi></math>
<math><mn>23</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>23</mn></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
and <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>81</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>25</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>10</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>81</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>25</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mn>6</mn></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>10</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>106</mn></math>
Hence, <math><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>106</mn></math>

</details>

### Q13. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The equation of the plane which passes through the point of intersection of <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> and <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> and perpendicular to <math><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>,</mo></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>4</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>50</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>4</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>25</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>4</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>4</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>50</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mn>4</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>50</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Any general point on the line <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> is</p><p><math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>2</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
This must satisfy the given <math><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi></mrow></msup></math> line</p><p><math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
So, the point of intersection of the two lines are <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
Equation of the required plane is</p><p><math><mn>4</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>16</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>9</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>25</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>50</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q14. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The equation of a plane containing the line formed by <math><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo></math> <math><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math> and at a distance of <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>6</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math> units from the point <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2,1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>62</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>29</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>19</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>105</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>62</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>29</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>19</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>105</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Equation of the required plane is
<math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>λ</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>λ</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>λ</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>λ</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>λ</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
Distance of the plane from <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2,1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>6</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>λ</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>λ</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>λ</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>λ</mi></mrow><msqrt><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>λ</mi></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>λ</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>λ</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></msqrt></mfrac></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msqrt><mn>6</mn></msqrt></mfrac></math>
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>λ</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>11</mn><msup><mrow><mi>λ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>12</mn><mi>λ</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>6</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mn>6</mn><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>λ</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>11</mn><msup><mrow><mi>λ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>12</mn><mi>λ</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>5</mn><msup><mi>λ</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>24</mn><mi>λ</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>⇒</mo><mi>λ</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>24</mn><mn>5</mn></mfrac></math>
Hence, the equation of the plane is
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>62</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>29</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>19</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>105</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

</details>

### Q15. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The image of the line <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> in the plane <math><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn></math> can be

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mi>λ</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>α</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>β</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>u</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>β</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Note that the line is parallel to the plane, hence the image of the line will be a parallel line.</p><p>Now,  let the image of <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2,3</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> in <math><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>Then, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mstyle displaystyle="true"><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mstyle><mstyle displaystyle="true"><mn>1</mn></mstyle></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mstyle displaystyle="true"><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mstyle><mstyle displaystyle="true"><mn>1</mn></mstyle></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></mfenced></math> </p><p>i.e. <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8801;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0,1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
So the required equation is <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math></p>

</details>

### Q16. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

If the planes <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>c</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>c</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>b</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> pass through a straight line, then the value of <math><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi><mi>c</mi></math> is

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **1**

**Detailed Solution:**

Given planes are:

<math><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>c</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> ...(i)

<math><mi>c</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> ...(ii)

<math><mi>b</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> ...(iii)

Equation of the plane passing through the line of intersection of the plane (i) and (ii) may be taken as:

<math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>c</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>λ</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>c</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<math><mi>o</mi><mi>r</mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi>x</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>λ</mi><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>c</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>λ</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>λ</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> ...(iv)

If plane (iii) and (iv) are the same, then 

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>∴</mo><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><mi>λ</mi></mrow><mi>b</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>λ</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow><mi>a</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>λ</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi> </mi><mi>or</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi>λ</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mi>c</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced></mfrac></math>

and <math><mi>λ</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>(</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mo>∴</mo><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mi>c</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mi>c</mi><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>b</mi><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>b</mi><mi>c</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi><msup><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi><msup><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mi>c</mi></math>

<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>b</mi><mi>c</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi><msup><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi><msup><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi>a</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi><mi>c</mi><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

</details>
