### Q17. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

Given four points <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>B</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>C</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math><mi>D</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>. Point <math><mi>D</mi></math> lies on the line <math><mi>L</mi></math> which is perpendicular to the plane determined by the points <math><mi>A</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>B</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>C</mi><mo>.</mo></math> If the point of intersection of the plane determined by the points <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>B</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>C</mi></math> and the line <math><mi>L</mi></math> is <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>b</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>, then <math><mn>9</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **46**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Equation of the plane through <math><mi>A</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>B</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>C</mi></math> is
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mspace linebreak="newline"></mspace><mo>⇒</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>z</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
Equation of the line through <math><mi>D</mi></math> and perpendicular to the plane is
<math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>∵</mo></math> Point <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>b</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is the point of intersection of the line and plane,</p><p>any general point on the line is <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>λ</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>4</mn><mi>λ</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>λ</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
It must satisfy the equation of the plane, so,
<math><mi>λ</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>16</mn><mi>λ</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>λ</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>⇒</mo><mn>18</mn><mi>λ</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi>λ</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>18</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
Hence, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>≡</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mn>5</mn><mn>18</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mn>38</mn><mn>18</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mn>49</mn><mn>18</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>⇒</mo><mn>9</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>46</mn></math></p>

</details>

---

### Q18. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Two intersecting lines lying in plane <math><msub><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math> have equations <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> and <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>.</mo></math> If the equation of plane <math><msub><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> is <math><mn>7</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> , then the distance between planes <math><msub><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math> and <math><msub><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>11</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Equation of plane <math><msub><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math> is <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mn>7</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
So, distance between planes <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>P</mi><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>&#160;</mo></mrow></msub><mo>&#38;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math> is <math><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>49</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>25</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

---

### Q19. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A plane <math><mi>P</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> passing through the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1,1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> is perpendicular to the planes <math><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math> and <math><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>.</mo></math> If the distance of the point <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1,2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> from the plane <math><mi>P</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> is <math><mi>k</mi></math> units, then the value of <math><mn>34</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><msup><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>17</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>16</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>64</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>128</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mn>128</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Normal vector to the plane <math><mi>P</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>6</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>10</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>10</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>15</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>10</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>10</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>15</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
Equation of the plane through <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1,1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> is
<math><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
So, the distance from the point <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1,2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> is <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>9</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>17</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>17</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math> units

</details>

---

### Q20. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

The equation of the plane passing through the point of intersection of the lines <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo></math> <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> and perpendicular to the line <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> is <math><mi>P</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo></math> If the distance of the point <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1,1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> from <math><mi>P</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> is <math><mi>k</mi></math> units, then the value of <math><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **7**

**Detailed Solution:**

<div>Any general point on the line <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> is <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>λ</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>λ</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2,2</mn><mi>λ</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></div>For the point of intersection, this point must satisfy the line <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>⇒</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>λ</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>λ</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>λ</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>⇒</mo><mi>λ</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
So, point of intersection is <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>4,3</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
The required equation of plane is
<math><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>⇒</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>22</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
Hence, the distance of the plane from <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> <math><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>22</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>14</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>14</mn></msqrt><mo>=</mo><mi>k</mi></math>

</details>

---

### Q21. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The direction ratios of the normal to the plane passing through the points <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and parallel to the line <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> are proportional to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>4</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>7</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Let, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>c</mi></math> be the DRs.</p><p>The equation of a plane passing through <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> is

<math><mi>a</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> .... (i)

Since, it passes through <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mi>&#160;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> and is parallel to the given line

<math><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>a</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

Also, <math><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>28</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>14</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

Hence, <math><mi>a</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>:</mo><mi>b</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>:</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>:</mo><mn>0</mn><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>:</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p>

</details>

---

### Q22. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A plane <math><mi>P</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> is the perpendicular bisector of the line joining the points <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2,3</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>6,7</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>.</mo></math> The perpendicular distance of <math><mi>P</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> from the origin is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>4</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></math> units
- **(B)** <math><mn>5</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></math> units
- **(C)** <math><mn>6</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></math> units
- **(D)** <math><mn>8</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></math> units

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mn>5</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></math> units**

**Detailed Solution:**

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/d12c3515-c757-4eea-b3b7-9672fa9be2ab.png" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width:1.87986in;height:1.08681in" />
From diagram mid-point of <math><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></math> must lie on the plane
<math><mi>M</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8801;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>4,5</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></math> is perpendicular to the required plane
Direction ratios of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>4,4</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>&#62;</mo><mo>=</mo><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#62;</mo></math>
Equation of the required plane is
<math><mn>1</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>15</mn></math>
The perpendicular distance from the origin to the plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mfrac><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>15</mn></mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mfrac></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></math> units

</details>

### Q23. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If the equation of the plane passing through <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and situated at a maximum distance from point <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> is <math><mi>P</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0,</mn></math> then the distance (in units) of <math><mi>P</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> from origin is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(C)** <math><msqrt><mn>6</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>3</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Let, the point <math><mi>A</mi></math> is <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>B</mi></math> is <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mo>&lt;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>1</mn><mo>&gt;</mo></math>
Required plane must be perpendicular to <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math>
So, the equation of the plane is <math><mn>1</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
Perpendicular distance (in units) of the point <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> from the plane is <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></math>

</details>

### Q24. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Equation of the plane passing through the point <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1,3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>, parallel to the vector <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math> and perpendicular to the plane <math><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn></math> is given by <math><mi>a</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>, then the value of <math><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn><mi>c</mi></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>32</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>31</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>184</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>72</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>184</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mo>∵</mo></math> required plane is passing through point <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1,3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
and parallel to vector <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math> and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
Therefore, the equation of plane is <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>4</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mn>10</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mn>16</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>24</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>32</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>16</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>24</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>32</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q25. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Equation of the plane passing through the point of intersection of the line <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> and <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> and perpendicular to the line <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>22</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>22</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>13</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>22</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Any general point on the line
<math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> is <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>λ</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>λ</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2,2</mn><mi>λ</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
For the point of intersection, this point must satisfy <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
Hence, <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>λ</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>λ</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>λ</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mn>6</mn><mi>λ</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>=</mo><mi>λ</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>⇒</mo><mi>λ</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
So, the point is <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>4,3</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
Also, a vector parallel to <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> is <math><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
Hence, the equation of the required plane is
<math><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>22</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

</details>

### Q26. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

If the magnitude of the projection of the vector <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math> on the vector perpendicular to the plane containing the vectors <math><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math> and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>k</mi></math>, then the value of <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **14.75**

**Detailed Solution:**

A vector perpendicular to the plane is <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
Let, <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
Projection of <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> on <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mfrac><mrow><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced></mfrac></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow><msqrt><mn>59</mn></msqrt></mfrac></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>59</mn></msqrt></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>k</mi></math>

</details>

### Q27. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A plane <math><mi>P</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> passes through the line of intersection of the planes <math><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>. If the plane <math><mi>P</mi></math> divides the line joining <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> in the ratio <math><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>:</mo><mn>1</mn></math> internally and the  equation of the plane is <math><mi>a</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>c</mi></math> where <math><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>∈</mo><mi>N</mi></math>, then the value of <math><mn>3</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>c</mi></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>22</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>32</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>42</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>10</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mn>32</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>The equation of the plane is <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>x</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>&#955;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>&#955;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>&#955;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
A point which divides <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> in <math><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>:</mo><mn>1</mn></math> internally is</p><p><math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>&#8801;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
This point must satisfy the equation of the plane</p><p><math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
Equation of the required plane is <math><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
Hence, <math><mn>3</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>18</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>24</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>32</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q28. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The plane containing the line <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> passes through the points <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>, then <math><mn>3</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>b</mi></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>4</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>16</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>16</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mo>-</mo><mn>16</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

If the plane contains the given line then it must pass through <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>b</mi><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and should be parallel to <math><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
Also, the plane passes through the points <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>.
Equation of the plane is
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>4</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>∴</mo></math> points <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>b</mi><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> satisfy the equation of the plane, if
<math><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>⇒</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
<math><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>⇒</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

</details>

### Q29. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>:</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math> and <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>:</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math> be two planes. Let <math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi></math> be two points in space. The equation of plane <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math> through the line of intersection of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math> and <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> such that the length of the projection upon it of the line segment <math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></math> is the least, is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>7</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>11</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>7</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Equation of plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></math> is <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi></math>
<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>3</mn><mo>&#62;</mo></math>
Length of projection of <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> on plane is least
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> must be perpendicular to the plane
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
So, the equation of the plane is <math><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>7</mn></math>

</details>

### Q30. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A plane <math><msub><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math> has the equation <math><mn>4</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math> and <math><msub><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> has the equation <math><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>k</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>7</mn></math>. If the angle between <math><msub><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math> and <math><msub><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> is <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>, then the value of <math><mi>k</mi></math> can be

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>17</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>17</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mn>17</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

A normal vector to plane <math><msub><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math> is <math><mo>&lt;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>1</mn><mo>&gt;</mo></math>
A normal vector to plane <math><msub><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> is <math><mo>&lt;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>k</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&gt;</mo></math>
Now, <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>k</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>6</mn></msqrt><msqrt><msup><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>k</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><msup><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>30</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mn>4</mn><msup><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>32</mn><mi>k</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>64</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><msup><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>30</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>32</mn><mi>k</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>34</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><msup><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>16</mn><mi>k</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>17</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>k</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>17</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>k</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi>k</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>17</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

</details>

### Q31. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Direction cosines of the normal to the plane containing the lines <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>5</mn></mfrac></math> are

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>14</mn></msqrt></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><msqrt><mn>14</mn></msqrt></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mn>5</mn><msqrt><mn>14</mn></msqrt></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>14</mn></msqrt></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><msqrt><mn>14</mn></msqrt></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msqrt><mn>14</mn></msqrt></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>14</mn></msqrt></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><msqrt><mn>14</mn></msqrt></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msqrt><mn>14</mn></msqrt></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>13</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>13</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>14</mn></msqrt></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><msqrt><mn>14</mn></msqrt></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msqrt><mn>14</mn></msqrt></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

The normal vector to the plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>5</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math>

Hence, direction cosines are <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>14</mn></msqrt></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><msqrt><mn>14</mn></msqrt></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msqrt><mn>14</mn></msqrt></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>

</details>

### Q32. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A perpendicular is drawn from a point on the line <math><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> to the plane <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math> such that the foot of the perpendicular <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Q</mi></math> also lies on the plane <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>. Then the coordinates of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Q</mi></math> are

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>–</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mo>–</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mn>2</mn><mi> </mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Let point <math><mi>P</mi></math> on the line is <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#955;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>The foot of perpendicular <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Q</mi></math> is given by
<math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>&#955;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>&#8757;</mo><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mi>Q</mi></math> Lies on <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mo>&#38;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>&#38;</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>Q</mi></math> is <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p>

</details>

### Q33. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Lying in the plane <math><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn></math> is a line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>L</mi></math> passing through <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1,2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and perpendicular to the line of intersection of planes <math><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn></math> and <math><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo></math> then the equation of <math><mi>L</mi></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Required line lies in the plane <math><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn></math> so it is perpendicular to <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math> and it is also perpendicular to line of intersection of the two given planes.
Let, <math><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math> &amp; <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>ν</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> be a vector along the required line
So, <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>ν</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>×</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>×</mo><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
Hence, equation of the line is <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q34. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The equation of the line which intersect each of the two lines <math><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>=</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>z</mi></math> and <math><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and is parallel to <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>4</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-1</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>+3</mo></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>4</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>4</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>+7</mo></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>4</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mn>4</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>+7</mo></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Equation of the first plane is <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>x</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>&#955;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#955;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
Equation of the second plane is <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>u</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>x</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>u</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>u</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>u</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>u</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
Required line must lie in both planes and parallel to the line <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>&#955;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#955;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#955;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>u</mi></mrow></mfenced><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>u</mi></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>u</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>8</mn><mi>u</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mi></mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>u</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
Equation of both planes is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
So the equation of the required line is
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn></math>

</details>

### Q35. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>:</mo><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>:</mo><mo> </mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> be two planes. Let <math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>7</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> be two points in space. The equation of a third plane <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math> through the line of intersection of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math> and <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> and parallel to <math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>9</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">2x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>9</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>13</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>13</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Equation of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math> is <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1,3</mn><mo>&#62;</mo></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></math> normal vector to plane <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math> is <math><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi><mo>&#62;</mo></math>
If <math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></math> is parallel to <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>9</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>14</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
Equation of required plane is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>13</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p>i.e. <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>13</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p>

</details>
