### Q36. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Consider a plane <math><mi>P</mi><mo>:</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math>, a line <math><mi>L</mi><mo>:</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> and a point <math><mi>A</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4,1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>. If the line <math><mi>L</mi></math> intersects plane <math><mi>P</mi></math> at <math><mi>B</mi></math> and the <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mi>y</mi></math> plane at <math><mi>C</mi></math>, then the area (in sq. units) of <math><mo>∆</mo><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><mi>C</mi></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><msqrt><mn>7</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(B)** <math><msqrt><mn>8</mn></msqrt><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi></math>
- **(C)** <math><msqrt><mn>10</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><msqrt><mn>10</mn></msqrt></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

For <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mi>y</mi></math> plane, putting <math><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>, we get,
<math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>⇒</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></math>
Coordinates of point <math><mi>C</mi></math> are <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>7</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>7,0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
Any general point on the line is <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>λ</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>λ</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>-</mo><mi>λ</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
This must satisfy the given plane
<math><mn>4</mn><mi>λ</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>λ</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>λ</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math>
<math><mn>2</mn><mi>λ</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>⇒</mo><mi>λ</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
So, coordinates of point <math><mi>B</mi></math> are <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>5</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4,1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mo>&lt;</mo><mn>2,0</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&gt;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>C</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mo>&lt;</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&gt;</mo></math>
Area <math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>C</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>4</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>10</mn></msqrt></math> sq. units

</details>

### Q37. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If the image of the point <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mtext> </mtext><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> in the plane <math><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>7</mn></math> is the point <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>α</mi><mo>,</mo><mtext> </mtext><mi>β</mi><mo>,</mo><mtext> </mtext><mi>γ</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>, then the value of <math><mi>α</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>β</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>γ</mi><mi> </mi></math>is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>10</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>8</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Since, <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>,</mo><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>,</mo><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mi>&#947;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>&#160;</mi></math>is the image of the point <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> in the plane. So, we have,

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mo>&#8757;</mo><mtext>Image&#160;of&#160;</mtext><mrow><mo>(</mo><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>)</mo></mrow><mtext>&#160;in&#160;the&#160;plane&#160;</mtext><mi>a</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>d</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>is</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>given</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>by</mi><mspace linebreak="newline"/><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8722;</mo><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mrow><mi>a</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>&#8722;</mo><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mrow><mi>b</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>&#8722;</mo><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mrow><mi>c</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>2</mn><mfrac><mrow><mo>(</mo><mi>a</mi><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>c</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#947;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#947;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>9</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#947;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

<math><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></math>

<math><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></math>

and <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#947;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#947;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math>

So, <math><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#947;</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></math>

</details>

---

### Q38. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> be a vector parallel to the line of intersection of the planes <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math> and <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>. The plane <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math> is parallel to the vectors <math><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math> and <math><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math> while plane <math><msub><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> is parallel to the vectors <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math> and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>. The acute angle between <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> and <math><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>12</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Let, <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>d</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
A normal vector perpendicular to the plane <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math> is <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math>
A normal vector perpendicular to the plane <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> is <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>d</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math>
A vector parallel to the line of intersection is <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#215;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>d</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
Let, <math><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi></math> be the acute angle between them then,
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mfrac><mrow><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#183;</mo><mover><mi>e</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q39. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The intercepts made on the <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>y</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>z</mi></math> axes, by the plane which bisects the line joining the points <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1,2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3,4</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> at right angles, are <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>b</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>c</mi></math> respectively, then the ordered triplet <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>,</mo><mn>9,9</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>,</mo><mn>9,9</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>9</mn><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mn>9</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>9</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mn>9</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>,</mo><mn>9,9</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Let, equation of the plane is <math><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>.</mo></math></p><p>Mid-point <math><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></math> of line joining <math><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1,2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3,4</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> is <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1,3</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>.</mo></math></p><p>It lies on the plane i.e.
<math><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
Also, <math><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi></math> is parallel to the normal of the plane, so,
<math><mfrac><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>18</mn></mfrac></math>
i.e. <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>9</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn></math>
Hence, ordered pair is <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>9,9</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p>

</details>

### Q40. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Consider the line <math><mi>L</mi><mi></mi><mo>:</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> and a family of planes <math><mi>P</mi></math> containing the line <math><mi>L</mi></math>. The member of the family of planes <math><mi>P</mi></math> which is situated at a maximum distance from <math><mi>A</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> will be

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>13</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>7</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>7</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>13</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>13</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Let the foot of the perpendicular from <math><mi>A</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> to given line <math><mi>L</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> is <math><mi>B</mi></math>, then the required plane must be perpendicular to <math><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></math> and contain the given line.
Any general point on the given line is <math><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>λ</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>λ</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>8</mn><mi>λ</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>10</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mo>&lt;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>λ</mi><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>λ</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>8</mn><mi>λ</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>&gt;</mo></math>
<math><mo>∴</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> is perpendicular to the given line
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>λ</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>λ</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>8</mn><mi>λ</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>10</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>λ</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>9</mn><mi>λ</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>64</mn><mi>λ</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>80</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi>λ</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
So, the coordinates of point <math><mi>B</mi></math> are <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
Equation of the required plane is
<math><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>10</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>20</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>26</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>13</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q41. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A line is drawn from the point <math><mi>P</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1,1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and perpendicular to a line whose direction ratios are <math><mn>1,1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></math> to intersect the plane <math><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math> at the point <math><mi>Q</mi><mo>.</mo></math> The locus of <math><mi>Q</mi></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>z</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Let point <math><mi>Q</mi></math> is <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><msub><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></math>
D.R&rsquo;s of <math><mi>P</mi><mi>Q</mi></math> are <math><msub><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><msub><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>1</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><msub><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>
Also <math><msub><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>
<math><msub><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>,</mo><msub><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><msub><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></math> is a point on the two planes
<math><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>
<math><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8756;</mo></math> the line on the intersection of these two planes will be the required locus</p><p>Now, the direction vector of the line is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math></p><p>Also, the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> satisfy both the equation of planes</p><p>Hence, the equation of the required line is <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math></p>

</details>

---

### Q42. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Image of line <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> in the plane <math><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>7</mn></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

The line is perpendicular to normal, hence the line is parallel to plane, so the reflected line has the same direction.
Now, image of <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2,1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> in the plane is <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>α</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>β</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>γ</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> say, then
<math><mfrac><mrow><mi>α</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>β</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>γ</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
So, the point is <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>4,3</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

</details>

### Q43. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The image of the line <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> in the plane <math><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math> meets the <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo></math>plane at the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> then the value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>c</mi></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>11</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>129</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>115</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>232</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>129</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Any general point on the line is <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>,</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1,3</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/4ab6a13e-6b27-4431-bf09-9ea5e1883eb2.png" style="width:1.33333in;height:1.17361in" />
On satisfying this point on the plane, we get, 
<math><mn>2</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>
<math><mn>13</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>13</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
So, coordinates of the point are <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>13</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>33</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>13</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>13</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
This point also lies on the image of the line
Image of point <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0,1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> also lies on the image of the line
<math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mfrac><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
Point is <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
Equation of image of the line is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>4</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></mrow><mn>28</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>7</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>5</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></mrow><mn>68</mn></mfrac></math>
For <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo></math>plane, putting <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>, we get, </p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>4</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></mrow><mn>28</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>7</mn><mn>24</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>5</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></mrow><mn>68</mn></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>129</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac></math></p>

</details>

### Q44. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A plane passes through <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and is perpendicular to two planes <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and  <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>.</mo></math> The distance of the plane from the point <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math> units
- **(B)** <math><mn>4</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></math> units
- **(C)** <math><mn>3</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></math> units
- **(D)** <math><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></math> units

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math> units**

**Detailed Solution:**

Normal vector perpendicular to the required plane is
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mi></mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
Equation of the plane through <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> is
<math><mn>1</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>0</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
Distance from <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> is <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q45. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><msub><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mi></mi></math> be the projection and <math><msub><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> be the image of <math><mi>z</mi></math>-axis in the plane <math><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo></math> The distance of the point <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1,2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> from the plane containing the lines <math><msub><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math> and <math><msub><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>2</mn></math> units
- **(B)** <math><mn>4</mn></math> units
- **(C)** <math><mn>5</mn></math> units
- **(D)** <math><mn>6</mn></math> units

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mn>2</mn></math> units**

**Detailed Solution:**

The required plane must contain <math><mi>z</mi></math>-axis and parallel to normal vector of the given plane
A parallel vector to <math><mi>z</mi></math>-axis is <math><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>0,0</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#62;</mo></math>
Normal vector to the given plane is <math><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4,1</mn><mo>&#62;</mo></math>
A normal vector to the required plane <math><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
<math><mo>&#8757;</mo><mi></mi></math> Required plane contain <math><mi>z</mi></math> axis, so it must pass through <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0,0</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
Equation of the required plane is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
So, the perpendicular distance from <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1,2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> to <math><mn>4</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> is <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math> units

</details>

---

### Q46. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The shortest distance between the line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>z</mi></math> and the line of intersection of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and <math><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math> units
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math> units
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>4</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math> units
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>5</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math> units

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math> units**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><msub><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><msub><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
The line of intersection is parallel to
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"></math>For the point on the line, putting <math><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>, we get,
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="}" open="" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
So, the required point is <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1,0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
Hence, the equation of the line of intersection is
<math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
Let, <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math>
<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>d</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>d</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
Shortest distance <math><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>⋅</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>d</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>d</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q47. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The projection of the line <math><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> on a plane <math><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi></math> is <math><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>, then the equation of the plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>11</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>35</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>–</mo><mn>21</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>45</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>11</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>=</mo><mn>35</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>–</mo><mn>8</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>21</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>45</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mn>5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>11</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>35</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

The required plane passes through <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>1,2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
The required plane is parallel to <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
So, normal vector to the plane is <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>11</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>11</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
Equation of the required plane is
<math><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>11</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>11</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>22</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>11</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>35</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mn>5</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>11</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>35</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

</details>

### Q48. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

If the plane <math><mi>a</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>d</mi></math> contains the line <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>d</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>, then the value of <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>d</mi></mrow></mfrac></math> is equal to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>b</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>d</mi><mo>&#8800;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **0.5**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Since the line lies on the given plane, therefore the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>d</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> on the line also lies on the plane</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>b</mi><mi>d</mi><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>c</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p>Also, the line will be perpendicular to the normal vector of the plane</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>c</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p>Hence, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>b</mi><mi>d</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mi>b</mi><mi>d</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mi>b</mi><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>d</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math></p>

</details>

### Q49. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Distance between two non-intersecting planes <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math> and <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> is <math><mn>5</mn></math> units, where <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math> is <math><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>26</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> is <math><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo></math> The point <math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> lies between the planes <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math> and <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>, then the value of <math><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>580</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>120</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>18</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>120</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mn>120</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Since both the planes are parallel
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>:</mo><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>12</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>52</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>:</mo><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>12</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
Therefore, <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>52</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>14</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>⇒</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>52</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>70</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>122</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>18</mn></math>
<math><mo>∴</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> is <math><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>12</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>122</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> or
<math><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>12</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>18</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
Since the point <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3,0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> is lying between <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math> and <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>
<math><mo>∴</mo></math> On substituting the point in both the equations of the plane, both the expressions must be of opposite signs
Hence, <math><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>12</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>18</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi></math> is the equation of the required plane

</details>

### Q50. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A plane passes through the point <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2,2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and contains the line joining the points <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1,2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1,1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>.</mo></math> Then the image of <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>7,2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> in the plane is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1,5</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>13</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>23</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>23</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>23</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

The plane passes through the points <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2,2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1,2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1,1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
Its equation is <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
Image of <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>7,2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> in the plane is
<math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>18</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>9</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>36</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>23</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>23</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></math>

</details>

### Q51. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mi>a</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>13</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> is a plane through the line of intersection of <math><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo></math> <math><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and is perpendicular to the plane <math><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>, then the value of <math><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>c</mi></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>12</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>12</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>10</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>10</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mo>-</mo><mn>10</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Equation of the plane through the line of intersection of given <math><mn>2</mn></math> planes is
<math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>λ</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>λ</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>λ</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>λ</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>λ</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>∵</mo></math> it is perpendicular to <math><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>
<math><mn>6</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>λ</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>λ</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>λ</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mn>6</mn><mi>λ</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>⇒</mo><mi>λ</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mn>7</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>13</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>9</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
On comparing <math><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>9</mn></math>

</details>

### Q52. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A ray of light through the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1,2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> strikes the plane <math><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>12</mn></math> at a point <math><mi>B</mi></math> and on reflection passes through the point <math><mi>C</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3,5</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>9</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>.</mo></math> If the equation of a plane containing the incident ray and the reflected ray is <math><mi>P</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and the distance of <math><mi>P</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> from <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0,0</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> is <math><mi>λ</mi></math> units, then the value of <math><mn>13</mn><msup><mrow><mi>λ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>4</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>6</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mn>2</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/070f1724-a4e8-44ca-99da-7c6a3ebf5b9c.png" style="width:2.09375in;height:1.16667in" />
A plane which contains the incident ray and the reflected ray must contain the points <math><mi>A</mi><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>B</mi><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>C</mi></math> and should be perpendicular to the given plane.
So, equation of the plane is <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>6</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
Hence, the distance of the plane from <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0,0</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> is <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>9</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>16</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>26</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math> units

</details>

### Q53. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>L</mi></math> be the line through the intersection of the planes <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>. Then, the equation of the plane passing through <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and perpendicular to the line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>L</mi></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>17</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

A vector parallel to the line of intersection is <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
Also, the line of intersection is perpendicular to the required plane.
Hence, equation of the plane is <math><mn>1</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

</details>
