### Q54. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If the planes <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math> and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math> intersect in a line, then the possible number of real values of <math><mi>a</mi></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>4</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mn>0</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mo>∵</mo></math> all <math><mn>3</mn></math> planes intersect in a line
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>a</mi></mtd><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>⇒</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
for both values of <math><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo></math> two of the given <math><mn>3</mn></math> planes are parallel (rejected)

</details>

### Q55. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>:</mo><mo> </mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>:</mo><mo> </mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and the equation of line <math><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></math> is <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> in <math><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi></math> space. Shortest distance between the line of intersection of planes <math><msub><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math> and <math><msub><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> and the line <math><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>10</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math> units
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>7</mn><msqrt><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mn>5</mn></mfrac></msqrt></math> units
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>10</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math> units
- **(D)** <math><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></math> units

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>7</mn><msqrt><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mn>5</mn></mfrac></msqrt></math> units**

**Detailed Solution:**

Plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></math> is parallel to line <math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo></math> Shortest distance <math><mo>=</mo></math> Perpendicular distance of any point on line to plane <math><msub><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mi></mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mfrac><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>9</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><msqrt><mn>9</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></msqrt></mfrac></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>14</mn><msqrt><mn>10</mn></msqrt></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>7</mn><msqrt><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mn>5</mn></mfrac></msqrt></math> units

</details>

### Q56. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>L</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>:</mo><mo> </mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>z</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>L</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>:</mo><mo> </mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></math> be two lines. The foot of perpendicular drawn from the origin <math><mi>O</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0,0</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> on <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>L</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></math> to <math><msub><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> is <math><mi>A</mi><mo>.</mo></math> If the equation of a plane containing the line <math><msub><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math> and perpendicular to <math><mi>O</mi><mi>A</mi></math> is <math><mn>10</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>d</mi></math>, then the value of <math><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>d</mi></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>10</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>10</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>12</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mo>-</mo><mn>10</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/2999ab7c-8cb0-452a-b08d-f999162deebd.png" style="width:2.9in;height:1.30694in" />
From diagram,
<math><mi>O</mi><mi>A</mi></math> is perpendicular to line <math><msub><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mn>1</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>λ</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>λ</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>λ</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>⇒</mo><mi>λ</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
So, coordinates of point <math><mi>A</mi></math> are <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1,0</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>A</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
<math><mo>∵</mo></math> A normal vector to required plane is <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>A</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math>
Equation of the required plane is <math><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mn>10</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>10</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>d</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

</details>

### Q57. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>:</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>:</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mo>:</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> be three planes, then the distance of the line of intersection of planes <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and <math><msub><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> from the plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>14</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math> units
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>14</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math> units
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>7</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math> units
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>7</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math> units

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>14</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math> units**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>A vector normal to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><msub><mi>n</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
A vector normal to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><msub><mi>n</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
Hence, the line of intersection is parallel to <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
And for point of intersection Put <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> in
<math><msub><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and <math><msub><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>, i.e.
<math><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mn>4</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>–</mo><mn>1</mn></math> and <math><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
So point is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1,0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
Equation of the line of intersection is
<math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
The line is parallel to <math><msub><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p>Hence, distance <math><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>14</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>14</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math></p>

</details>

### Q58. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>:</mo><mo> </mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>:</mo><mo> </mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> be two planes. The equation of the plane perpendicular to the line of intersection of <math><msub><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and <math><msub><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and passing through <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1,1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>11</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>13</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>7</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=13</mo></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>7</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=13</mo></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>The normal vector of plane <math><msub><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> is <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
The normal vector of plane <math><msub><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><msub><mi>n</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
A vector parallel to the line of intersection of <math><msub><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and <math><msub><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> is</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>7</mn><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
It is also a normal vector for the required plane
Hence, the equation of the required plane is
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>7</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>7</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>13</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q59. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The equation of the plane through the points <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1,0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4,5</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and parallel to a line with direction cosines proportional to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2,3</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn></math> is <math><mn>9</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>k</mi></math> , then the value of <math><mi>k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>20</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>20</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>10</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>10</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mn>20</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Equation of the plane through <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1,0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> is
<math><mi>a</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
It also passes through <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4,5</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> then
<math><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>... (i)
Given plane is parallel to a line with direction cosines proportional to <math><mn>2,3</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>4</mn></math>, so 
<math><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>... (ii)
Solving equations (i) and (ii),
<math><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>12</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>15</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>10</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>27</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>9</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>λ</mi></math>
<math><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>9</mn><mi>λ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">λ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">λ</mi></math>
So, the equation of the plane is
<math><mo>-</mo><mn>9</mn><mi>λ</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>λ</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>λ</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>-</mo><mn>9</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>18</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>-</mo><mn>9</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>20</mn></math>
<math><mn>9</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>20</mn></math>
On comparison, <math><mi>k</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>20</mn></math>

</details>

---
